`docs/blueprint/assessment/*.json`. **Do not edit by hand.** This is the live
register (ADR 0099), and there is no second one.

| R12-LEDGER-5011 | Ledger records settlement state | Settlement enum has exactly one variant, Simulated (backend/crates/libs/qip-contracts/src/ledger.rs:42-44), by design (paper boundary); test settlement_has_no_variant_but_simulated. No pending/settled lifecycle exists. Two production facts about settlement exist and neither is a settlement the ledger could record: the simulated broker books cash at the fill and reports no settlement, and the desk's settlement register (Platform::register_settlement, T+1 from the SettlementBook declared at assembly) holds a calendar projection of when sale proceeds become usable, read by survival sizing and never confirmed by anything. | PARTIAL | A simulated settlement lifecycle (pending -> settled, T+n) without introducing a live variant. No producer observes a settlement: the venue settles at the fill and the desk's T+1 register is a projection, so recording 'settled' would be the kernel's clock passing a calendar's estimate, not a reported fact. A lifecycle needs an in-settlement subledger the fill posts into and a settled transition the ledger books, which changes how fills post cash today and is an ADR, not a producer. |  | P0 | cargo test -p qip-ledgerd --test store plus a new test per state |  |
| R12-LEDGER-5012 | Every posting cites the observed fact that caused it | SourceEvent names the cell, session, journal sequence and digest, and the order; LedgerEvent::new refuses an empty cell, digest or order id, built in code or deserialised, with a field-by-field test. qip-ledgerd's post copies the source from the outcome it was given. | COMPLETE | None for the requirement; not deployed. |  | P0 | cargo test -p qip-contracts --lib ledger |  |
| R12-LEDGER-5013 | Global journal with idempotent event IDs | Ledgerd consumer (now also verifying lineage) applies each record once; same position+digest is a no-op, different content parks the cell. | COMPLETE | None. | R12-LEDGER-5001 | P0 | cargo test -p qip-ledgerd --test store |  |
| R12-LEDGER-5014 | Immutable lineage from intent to cash impact | LedgerEvent::chain_correlation derives the chain id from source (cell, session, order) and Decision::ChainOpened ties order to signal in the journal; SourceEvent still has no intent id and settlement is a state, not a record. | PARTIAL | Intent id and settlement record on the ledger event; a balance-to-intent walk in both directions. A settlement record has nothing to carry: no production path observes a settlement (the simulated venue settles at the fill; the desk's T+1 register is a projection), so the walk still stops at order/signal. Treasury and capital-grant fundings now reach the ledger as operations whose source cites the operation id and the desk chain, so a funding balance walks back to the grant's own identity (strategy@cell@granted-at) and the journalled CapitalGrantFunded record; that is a lineage for a funding, not the intent -> order -> fill -> settlement chain this row names. |  | P0 | new acceptance test walking ledger -> journal -> intent |  |
| R12-LEDGER-5015 | Ledger has a trading subledger | qip-ledgerd main -> start_with_link -> PartitionWorker -> LedgerStore::apply -> qip_portfolio::ledger::post books Account::Trading postings; the composed-ledger test asserts literal trading/venue/fees balances and a zero trial balance per unit. | COMPLETE | None for the subledger clause; deployment tracked separately. |  | P1 | cargo test -p qip-ledgerd plus a subledger test |  |
| R12-LEDGER-5016 | Ledger has a treasury subledger | Account::Treasury is the credit side of two production operations that reach qip-ledgerd over ledger.operation_recorded: every desk's treasury funding, queued at assembly (Platform::queue_treasury_funding), and every capital grant's funding, queued when two operators issue the grant (Platform::issue_capital -> fund_capital_grant, OperationKind::CapitalGrantFunded). Both post Dr the book's trading account, Cr treasury:central; the trial balance closes in the acceptance test against a real ledgerd, and the treasury balance is the sum of both fundings. A grant's funding is recalled once — Dr treasury, Cr the book, for the funding's own deployable capital, as OperationKind::CapitalGrantRecalled with cause expired, revoked or superseded — by the cycle that observes its window has ended (Platform::recall_lapsed_capital_grants, ACT), by the ingestion whose recall order withdraws it (Platform::recall_revoked_capital_grant), or by the renewal that funds the same book under a later grant (Platform::recall_superseded_capital_grant, recall before funding), so the book's deployable capital at the ledger is the live grants' sum; the acceptance test shows a real ledgerd returning the book to zero and the treasury to the desk's own funding at expiry. A restart replays each funding and recall record as written, rebuilds which fundings are outstanding from the same walk, and the ledger answers each resend with a duplicate. | COMPLETE |  |  | P1 | cargo test -p qip-kernel --test central -- grant recall; cargo test -p qip-acceptance --test vertical_slice_ship -- funding recall; cargo test -p qip-contracts --test ledger_operation |  |

| R12-LEDGER-5018 | Ledger has a physical inventory/commerce subledger | Account::Inventory and an inventory-move OperationKind make physical inventory a subledger of the global journal; qip_financial::commerce::Ledger still posts nothing to it. | PARTIAL | No production producer for this kind. Two other kinds now reach qip-ledgerd from production (treasury funding at every desk start, capital-grant funding at issue; ADR 0123 decisions 5 and 6), so the path exists; what is missing is an observer of a physical movement that runs by default. Receipts into a warehouse and sales to the paper buyer are observed by the commerce research plane (qip-kernel/src/commerce_research.rs, LedgerEvent::Received and CommercePlane::sell) only when the deep brain is handed a breadth plan (QIP_DEEPBRAIN_BREADTH_PLAN), which no committed environment sets; the journaled CommerceSimulated record also carries no warehouse on its purchases and no unit id on its sales, so a movement could not be replayed from the log as written without journaling the operation itself. |  | P2 | cargo test -p qip-ledgerd plus a subledger test |  |

| R12-EVID-2031 | Every extracted claim becomes an Evidence object with full provenance | Every connector-admitted record gets an EvidenceRecord (source identity, timestamps, location, extraction method, authenticity signal, licence, confidence) built by evidence_of (connector/evidence.rs:150-196); EvidenceRecord::new refuses blank source, method, licence or statement (qip-evidence/src/evidence.rs:62-118); an event whose evidence cannot be formed or is on an impossible timeline is quarantined (connector/runtime.rs:918-970). The evidence is now forwarded into the world model's fabric by Platform::absorb_evidence (evidence_intake.rs:103-150) from qip-fastbrain node.rs:137 and qip-deepbrain connectors.rs:365. But the record still enters the world model via platform.observe(batch.accepted) (fastbrain node.rs:138) independently of whether the world fabric admitted its evidence (refusals are only counted, evidence_intake.rs:140-147), the observed facts carry no link to their evidence id, and non-connector feeds (synthetic, replay, tape, live) forward no evidence at all (fastbrain feed.rs:580-588). | PARTIAL | Gate a record's entry into the world model on its evidence being admitted (or link each world-model fact to its evidence id), wrap claims from the live/tape feeds, and a test that fails when a claim reaches the world model without evidence. | R12-DATA-2012 | P0 | an integration test that a narrative poll produces EvidenceRecords |  |
| R12-EVID-2032 | Evidence links to corroborating and contradicting evidence | EvidenceFabric::admit links a new claim to held claims of the same key as corroborates/contradicts and writes the ids onto the new record and Corroborates/Contradicts edges into the provenance graph (fabric.rs:657-765). The world fabric is now fed in production (evidence_intake.rs:103-150). Links are recorded on the new claim only: the earlier claim's corroborates/contradicts lists are not updated (fabric.rs:760-765 push onto `record`, the incoming one). The fabric is in-memory, bounded and evicts (fabric.rs:812-838); nothing journals it, and the intake module states durability is not claimed (evidence_intake.rs:33-38). | PARTIAL | Record the relation on both records, persist links to the event log, and a replay test proving links survive. | R12-EVID-2031 | P1 | cargo test -p qip-world-model evidence |  |

The world fabric is now fed by both brains (evidence_intake.rs:103). Nothing links a belief (NOW variable, thesis, world-model fact) into the graph, and sources_of/graph() have no production reader (grep for sources_of( and .graph() outside qip-evidence finds none in kernel/apps). | PARTIAL | Link beliefs to the claims they rest on and provide/test a walk from a belief to every source document hash. | R12-EVID-2032 | P1 | a test walking a belief back to source hashes |  |

| R12-AGENCY-1006 | Causal agency: simulate authorized interventions and learn effect | Stage ladder via CLI shadow; deepbrain drafts to outbox through the guard. After a shadowed pass with a structural model, qip-cli calls engine::learn_shadowed on the pass's simulated outcome: observe-effect, attribute and update run, the attribution is journaled (AgencyRecord::Learned) and the action policy is held to its predicted uplift. With no action policy the engine executes nothing (Act is shadowed). On the deepbrain node (the default path), AmbientAgency holds a draft until a later sweep raises its subject again, then runs ObserveEffect, Attribute and Update through qip_agency::engine::learn on a measured outcome: the subject's reading at the follow-up sweep against its reading when drafted (status quo as the no-action baseline), kept in ActionOutcomeMemory as Observed::Measured, the Communication policy held to a predicted uplift of zero, the attribution recorded NotIdentified at identifiability 0, and the record journaled as DraftLearning (or withheld with the stage that refused it). | PARTIAL | The effect learned on the default path is measured but not identified: no control exists for a draft, ADR 0114 refuses an experiment on the people it reaches, so identifiability is 0 and the attribution says so; a measured *causal* effect needs an identified estimator, and none can run on this seam. |  | P2 | cargo test -p qip-agency; cargo test -p qip-cli (agency shadow); cd backend && cargo +1.94.1 test -p qip-deepbrain --test ambient_agency |  |

| R12-SEC-8100 | Short-lived workload certificates via CAS; KMS/HSM roots; IAM-mapped identities | mTLS exists in code. qip-transport/src/tls.rs (ADR 0112, rustls/ring, TLS 1.3 only) builds a server config that always requires a client certificate chained to the configured roots (tls.rs:132), plus a PeerNames SAN allow-list (tls.rs:212). qip-fabricd serves with it when configured (apps/qip-fabricd/src/lib.rs:517), and qip-ledgerd and qip-edge-node choose LinkSecurity (ledgerd main.rs:40, edge-node main.rs:997). KMS signing of policy, halt and envelope artefacts exists as qip_transport::kms::KmsSigner, selected in qip-api main.rs:306 when QIP_ARTEFACT_SIGNER=kms. CAS is declared in infrastructure/terraform/security.tf:111-216 (30-day leaf-only pool, requester IAM) but is null in every environment. | PARTIAL | (1) CAS is off everywhere and nothing issues certificates to a workload. No startup template or Terraform fetches a CAS leaf, and the execution-node test sets QIP_TLS_MODE=plaintext (modules/execution-node/tests/deployment.tftest.hcl:54). (2) Broker identity is not mapped to a GCP service account/IAM: the bearer token is still the authorisation (tls.rs:38-41). (3) Policy keys are not KMS-held anywhere: Terraform declares no ADR 0110 policy/halt/envelope ASYMMETRIC_SIGN key (only the Binary Authorization attestor, modules/binaryauthorization/main.tf:57), and the default signer is still the shared HMAC key until 2027-01-31 (libs/qip-transport/src/trust.rs:99). |  | P0 | integration test |  |
